2026-10-11T19:15:04.8827247Z  	 -- Row locking needs UPDATE on immutable versions, which runtime must not have.

2026-10-11T19:15:04.8827680Z  	 IF pg_catalog.current_setting('transaction_isolation') <> 'read committed' THEN
2026-10-11T19:15:04.8827944Z  	  RAISE EXCEPTION 'publication history writes require read committed' USING ERRCODE='25001';

2026-10-11T19:15:04.8828464Z  	  'weaveos:publication-history:' || NEW.app_id::text || ':' || NEW.flow_id::text,0));

2026-10-11T19:15:04.8829009Z  	 IF NOT FOUND THEN
2026-10-11T19:15:04.8829234Z  	  RAISE EXCEPTION 'publication history definition missing' USING ERRCODE='23503';

2026-10-11T19:15:04.8829937Z  	 IF NOT FOUND THEN
2026-10-11T19:15:04.8830278Z  	  RAISE EXCEPTION 'publication history version missing' USING ERRCODE='23503';
2026-10-11T19:15:04.8830357Z  	 END IF;
2026-10-11T19:15:04.8830475Z  	 IF TG_TABLE_NAME='workflow_publications' THEN
2026-10-11T19:15:04.8830762Z  	  IF NEW.view_id IS DISTINCT FROM original_view OR NEW.version_id IS DISTINCT FROM original_version THEN
2026-10-11T19:15:04.8830973Z  	   RAISE EXCEPTION 'publication history scope mismatch' USING ERRCODE='23503';
2026-10-11T19:15:04.8831157Z  	  END IF;

2026-10-11T19:15:04.8832241Z  	 RETURN NEW;
